    public interface IJobConfigurator<[System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.DynamicallyAccessedMembers(System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.DynamicallyAccessedMemberTypes.None | System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.DynamicallyAccessedMemberTypes.PublicParameterlessConstructor | System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.DynamicallyAccessedMemberTypes.PublicConstructors | System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.DynamicallyAccessedMemberTypes.PublicProperties | System.Diagnostics.CodeAnalysis.DynamicallyAccessedMemberTypes.Interfaces)]  TJob>
        where TJob : Quartz.IJob
    {
        Quartz.IJobConfigurator<TJob> DisallowConcurrentExecution(bool concurrentExecutionDisallowed = true);
        Quartz.IJobConfigurator<TJob> PersistJobDataAfterExecution(bool persistJobDataAfterExecution = true);
        Quartz.IJobConfigurator<TJob> RequestRecovery(bool shouldRecover = true);
        Quartz.IJobConfigurator<TJob> StoreDurably(bool durability = true);

Description, string TriggerType, Quartz.TriggerState State, System.DateTimeOffset StartTimeUtc, System.DateTimeOffset? EndTimeUtc, System.DateTimeOffset? NextFireTimeUtc, System.DateTimeOffset? PreviousFireTimeUtc, string? CalendarName, int Priority, string? ExecutionGroup) { }

    {

        new System.DateTimeOffset? NextFireTimeUtc { get; set; }

        new System.DateTimeOffset? PreviousFireTimeUtc { get; set; }
        new int Priority { get; set; }
        new System.DateTimeOffset StartTimeUtc { get; set; }
    }

    public abstract class TriggerBase : Quartz.Extensibility.IMutableTrigger, Quartz.Extensibility.IOperableTrigger, Quartz.ITrigger, System.IEquatable<Quartz.Impl.Triggers.TriggerBase>
    {

        public virtual bool HasAdditionalProperties { get; }
        protected abstract bool HasMillisecondPrecision { get; }
        public virtual Quartz.JobDataMap JobDataMap { get; set; }
        public Quartz.JobKey JobKey { get; set; }
        public Quartz.TriggerKey Key { get; set; }
        public abstract bool MayFireAgain { get; }
        public virtual int MisfireInstructionCode { get; set; }
        public abstract System.DateTimeOffset? NextFireTimeUtc { get; set; }
        public Quartz.PreferredNode PreferredNode { get; set; }
        public abstract System.DateTimeOffset? PreviousFireTimeUtc { get; set; }
        public virtual int Priority { get; set; }
        public virtual System.DateTimeOffset StartTimeUtc { get; set; }
        public virtual Quartz.ITrigger Clone() { }
        public abstract System.DateTimeOffset? ComputeFirstFireTimeUtc(Quartz.ICalendar? calendar);
        public virtual bool Equals(Quartz.Impl.Triggers.TriggerBase? trigger) { }
        public override bool Equals(object? obj) { }
        public virtual Quartz.SchedulerInstruction ExecutionComplete(Quartz.IJobExecutionContext context, Quartz.JobExecutionException? result) { }
        public abstract System.DateTimeOffset? GetFireTimeAfter(System.DateTimeOffset? afterTime);
        public override int GetHashCode() { }
        public abstract Quartz.IScheduleBuilder GetScheduleBuilder();
        public Quartz.TriggerBuilder<Quartz.IJob> GetTriggerBuilder() { }
        public override string ToString() { }
        public abstract void Triggered(Quartz.ICalendar? calendar);
        public abstract void UpdateAfterMisfire(Quartz.ICalendar? calendar);
        public abstract void UpdateWithNewCalendar(Quartz.ICalendar calendar, System.TimeSpan misfireThreshold);

namespace Quartz.Serialization.SystemTextJson.Calendars
{
    public abstract class CalendarSerializer<TCalendar> : Quartz.Serialization.SystemTextJson.Calendars.ICalendarSerializer
        where TCalendar : Quartz.ICalendar
    {
        protected CalendarSerializer() { }
        public abstract string CalendarTypeName { get; }
        protected abstract TCalendar Create(System.Text.Json.JsonElement jsonElement, System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions options);
        protected abstract void DeserializeFields(TCalendar calendar, System.Text.Json.JsonElement jsonElement, System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions options);
        protected abstract void SerializeFields(System.Text.Json.Utf8JsonWriter writer, TCalendar calendar, System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions options);
    }
    public interface ICalendarSerializer
    {
        string CalendarTypeName { get; }
        Quartz.ICalendar Create(System.Text.Json.JsonElement jsonElement, System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions options);
        void DeserializeFields(Quartz.ICalendar calendar, System.Text.Json.JsonElement jsonElement, System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions options);
        void SerializeFields(System.Text.Json.Utf8JsonWriter writer, Quartz.ICalendar calendar, System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions options);
    }
}
namespace Quartz.Serialization.SystemTextJson
{
    public sealed class SystemTextJsonSerializerRegistry
    {
        public SystemTextJsonSerializerRegistry() { }
        public Quartz.Serialization.SystemTextJson.SystemTextJsonSerializerRegistry AddCalendarSerializer<TCalendar>(Quartz.Serialization.SystemTextJson.Calendars.CalendarSerializer<TCalendar> serializer)
            where TCalendar : Quartz.ICalendar { }
        public Quartz.Serialization.SystemTextJson.SystemTextJsonSerializerRegistry AddTriggerSerializer<TTrigger>(Quartz.Serialization.SystemTextJson.Triggers.ITriggerSerializer serializer)
            where TTrigger : Quartz.ITrigger { }
        public Quartz.Serialization.SystemTextJson.SystemTextJsonSerializerRegistry AddTypeInfoResolver(System.Text.Json.Serialization.Metadata.IJsonTypeInfoResolver resolver) { }
    }
}

    public interface ITriggerSerializer
    {
        string TriggerTypeName { get; }
        Quartz.IScheduleBuilder CreateScheduleBuilder(System.Text.Json.JsonElement jsonElement, System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions options);
        void DeserializeFields(Quartz.ITrigger trigger, System.Text.Json.JsonElement jsonElement, System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions options);
        void SerializeFields(System.Text.Json.Utf8JsonWriter writer, Quartz.ITrigger trigger, System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions options);
    }
    public class RecurrenceTriggerSerializer : Quartz.Serialization.SystemTextJson.Triggers.TriggerSerializer<Quartz.Impl.Triggers.RecurrenceTriggerImpl>

    {

        protected override void SerializeFields(System.Text.Json.Utf8JsonWriter writer, Quartz.Impl.Triggers.SimpleTriggerImpl trigger, System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions options) { }
    }
    public abstract class TriggerSerializer<TTrigger> : Quartz.Serialization.SystemTextJson.Triggers.ITriggerSerializer
        where TTrigger : Quartz.ITrigger
    {
        protected TriggerSerializer() { }
        public abstract string TriggerTypeName { get; }
        public abstract Quartz.IScheduleBuilder CreateScheduleBuilder(System.Text.Json.JsonElement jsonElement, System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions options);
        protected virtual void DeserializeFields(TTrigger trigger, System.Text.Json.JsonElement jsonElement, System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions options) { }
        protected abstract void SerializeFields(System.Text.Json.Utf8JsonWriter writer, TTrigger trigger, System.Text.Json.JsonSerializerOptions options);
    }
